"range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "1", "solution_html": {"en": "\n

Shortcut Trick\n

Total Surface Area of a cube = 6 × a2

\n

Substitute the given edge a = 19 cm directly into the formula.

\n

TSA = 6 × 192 = 6 × 361

\n

TSA = 2166 cm2

\n

∴ The correct answer is 2166 cm2.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Edge of the cube (a) = 19 cm

\n

Formula Used:

\n

Total Surface Area (TSA) of a cube = 6a2

\n\n\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ TSA = 6 × (19)2

\n

⇒ TSA = 6 × 361

\n

⇒ TSA = 2166 cm2

\n

∴ The correct answer is 2166 cm2.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Lateral Surface Area of a Cube

\n

The lateral surface area includes only the 4 vertical side faces, excluding the top and bottom. LSA = 4a2.

\n

Volume of a Cube

\n

The total 3D space occupied by the cube is given by Volume = a3.

\n

Diagonals of a Cube

\n

Face Diagonal = a√2 | Body Diagonal = a√3, which represents the longest rod that can be perfectly placed inside the cube.

"\n

Shortcut Trick\n

Using the Net Deviation Method for Averages:

\n

Teacher's Weight = Old Average + (New Total People × Increase in Average)

\n

Teacher's Weight = 53 + (28 × 0.7)

\n

Teacher's Weight = 53 + 19.6 = 72.6 kg

\n

∴ The correct answer is 72.6 kg.

\n

Alternate Method

\n\n

Given:

\n

Number of students = 27

\n

Average weight of 27 students = 53 kg

\n

Average increase when teacher is included = 700 grams = 0.7 kg

\n

Formula Used:

\n

Total Weight = Average × Number of entities

\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ Total weight of 27 students = 27 × 53 = 1431 kg

\n

⇒ New number of people (students + teacher) = 28

\n

⇒ New average weight = 53 + 0.7 = 53.7 kg

\n

⇒ Total weight of 28 people = 28 × 53.7 = 1503.6 kg

\n

⇒ Weight of the teacher = (Total weight of 28 people) − (Total weight of 27 students)

\n

⇒ Weight of the teacher = 1503.6 − 1431 = 72.6 kg

\n

∴ The correct answer is 72.6 kg.

\n

Additional Information

\n

Combined Average

\n

When two groups of sizes n1 and n2 have averages A1 and A2, the combined average is (n1A1 + n2A2) ÷ (n1 + n2).

\n

Net Deviation Method

\n

If a new value joins a group and changes the average, the new value = Old Average + (Total Number of people × Change in Average). A positive change means the new value is higher than the old average.

\n

Replacement in Averages

\n

When one person is replaced by another, the weight of the new person = Weight of the removed person + (Total Number of people × Change in Average).

27 विद्यार्थ्यांचे सरासरी वजन = 53 किलो.

\n\n

\n\n

नवीन सरासरी = 53 + 0.7 = 53.7 किलो

\n\n

नवीन एकूण वजन = 28 × 53.7

\n\n

આપેલ છે:

\n\n

27 વિદ્યાર્થીઓનું સરેરાશ વજન = 53 કિગ્રા.

\n\n

શિક્ષકનું વજન સરેરાશ 700 ગ્રામ (0.7 કિગ્રા) વધે છે.

\n\n

શિક્ષક સહિત વિદ્યાર્થીઓની કુલ સંખ્યા = 28.

\n\n

ઉપયોગમાં લેવાયેલ સૂત્ર:

\n\n

શિક્ષકનું વજન = (નવી સરેરાશ x કુલ સભ્યો) - (જૂની સરેરાશ x પ્રારંભિક સભ્યો)

\n\n

ગણતરી:

\n\n

નવી સરેરાશ = 53 + 0.7 = 53.7 કિગ્રા

\n\n

જૂનું કુલ વજન = 27 x 53

\n\n

જૂનું કુલ વજન = 1431 કિગ્રા

\n\n

નવું કુલ વજન = 28 x 53.7

\n\n

નવું કુલ વજન = 1503.6 કિગ્રા

\n\n

શિક્ષકનું વજન = નવું કુલ વજન - જૂનું કુલ વજન

\n\n

⇒ શિક્ષકનું વજન = 1503.6 - 1431

\n\n

⇒ શિક્ષકનું વજન = 72.6 કિગ્રા

\n\n

\n\n

27 ವಿದ್ಯಾರ್ಥಿಗಳ ಸರಾಸರಿ ತೂಕ = 53 ಕೆ.ಜಿ.

\n\n

ಶಿಕ್ಷಕರ ತೂಕವನ್ನು ಸೇರಿಸಿದಾಗ ಸರಾಸರಿ 700 ಗ್ರಾಂ (0.7 ಕೆ.ಜಿ.) ಹೆಚ್ಚಾಗುತ್ತದೆ.

\n\n

ಶಿಕ್ಷಕರನ್ನು ಒಳಗೊಂಡಂತೆ ಒಟ್ಟು ವಿದ್ಯಾರ್ಥಿಗಳ ಸಂಖ್ಯೆ = 28.

\n\n

ಬಳಸಲಾದ ಸೂತ್ರ:

\n\n

ಶಿಕ್ಷಕರ ತೂಕ = (ಹೊಸ ಸರಾಸರಿ x ಒಟ್ಟು ಸದಸ್ಯರು) - (ಹಳೆಯ ಸರಾಸರಿ x ಆರಂಭಿಕ ಸದಸ್ಯರು)

\n\n

ಲೆಕ್ಕಾಚಾರ:

\n\n

ಹೊಸ ಸರಾಸರಿ = 53 + 0.7 = 53.7 ಕೆ.ಜಿ.

\n\n

ಹಳೆಯ ಒಟ್ಟು ತೂಕ = 27 x 53

\n\n

ಹಳೆಯ ಒಟ್ಟು ತೂಕ = 1431 ಕೆ.ಜಿ.

\n\n

ಹೊಸ ಒಟ್ಟು ತೂಕ = 28 x 53.7

\n\n

ಹೊಸ ಒಟ್ಟು ತೂಕ = 1503.6 ಕೆ.ಜಿ.

\n\n

ಶಿಕ್ಷಕರ ತೂಕ = ಹೊಸ ಒಟ್ಟು ತೂಕ - ಹಳೆಯ ಒಟ್ಟು ತೂಕ

\n\n

⇒ ಶಿಕ್ಷಕರ ತೂಕ = 1503.6 - 1431

\n\n

⇒ ಶಿಕ್ಷಕರ ತೂಕ = 72.6 ಕೆ.ಜಿ.

\n\n

ಶಿಕ್ಷಕರ ತೂಕ 72.6 ಕೆ.ಜಿ. ಆಗಿದೆ.

{"prompt": "4", "value": "6"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "4", "solution_html": {"en": "\n

Shortcut Trick\n

Difference in principal = ₹9400 − ₹5400 = ₹4000

\n

This extra principal generates the extra interest of ₹960 over 4 years.

\n

Rate (R) = (Difference in Interest × 100) ÷ (Difference in Principal × Time)

\n

R = (960 × 100) ÷ (4000 × 4)

\n

R = 96000 ÷ 16000 = 6

\n

∴ The correct answer is 6%.

\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Mukund's Principal (P1) = ₹5400

\n

Sachin's Principal (P2) = ₹9400

\n

Time (T) = 4 years

\n

Difference in Simple Interest = ₹960

\n

Formula Used:

\n

Simple Interest (SI) = (P × R × T) ÷ 100

\nदिया गया है:

\n\n

मुकुंद द्वारा निवेशित मूलधन = ₹5400

\n\n

सचिन द्वारा निवेशित मूलधन = ₹9400

\n\n

समय = 4 वर्ष

\n\n

ब्याज में अंतर = ₹960

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

साधारण ब्याज (SI) = (मूलधन × दर × समय) / 100

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

माना, प्रति वर्ष ब्याज दर R है।

\n\n

मुकुंद के लिए साधारण ब्याज: SIM = (5400 × R × 4) / 100

\n\n

ਕੀ ਹੈ?", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "45,362"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "48,200"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "40,230"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "43,792"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "4", "solution_html": {"en": "\n

Shortcut Trick\n

Loss = 44% = 44 ÷ 100 = 11 ÷ 25

\n

Cost Price (CP) = 25 units, Loss = 11 units

\n

Selling Price (SP) = CP − Loss = 25 − 11 = 14 units

\n

25 units = 78200 ⇒ 1 unit = 78200 ÷ 25 = 3128

\n

Selling Price (14 units) = 14 × 3128 = 43792

\n

∴ The correct answer is ₹43,792.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Cost Price (CP) of the laptop = ₹78,200

\n

Loss Percentage = 44%

\n

Formula Used:

\n

Selling Price (SP) = CP × [ (100 − Loss%) ÷ 100 ]

\n\n\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ SP = 78200 × [ (100 − 44) ÷ 100 ]

\n

⇒ SP = 78200 × (56 ÷ 100)

\n

⇒ SP = 78200 × 0.56

\n

⇒ SP = 782 × 56

\n

⇒ SP = 43792

\n

∴ The correct answer is ₹43,792.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Profit and Loss Basic Formulas

\n

Profit = SP − CP | Loss = CP − SP.

\n

Profit and Loss Percentage

\n

Profit% = (Profit ÷ CP) × 100 | Loss% = (Loss ÷ CP) × 100. Always remember that both profit and loss percentages are calculated on the Cost Price by default.

\n

Marked Price & Discount

\n

Discount is always offered on the Marked Price (MP). To find the selling price after a discount: SP = MP × (100 − Discount%) ÷ 100.

"end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "4", "solution_html": {"en": "\n

Shortcut Trick\n

Let the initial price of fuel be 100.

\n

Applying successive percentage multiplying factors:

\n

Final Price = 100 × 0.85 × 0.60 × 0.40 × 1.45 = 29.58

\n

Net percentage decrease = 100 − 29.58 = 70.42%

\n

∴ The correct answer is Decreases by 70.42%.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Successive changes in fuel price: −15%, −40%, −60%, +45%

\n

Formula Used:

\n

Final Value = Initial Value × (1 ± R1/100) × (1 ± R2/100) × ...

\n\n\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ Let the original price of fuel be 100.

\n

⇒ Price after 1st month (15% decrease) = 100 × (1 − 15/100) = 85

\n

⇒ Price after 2nd month (40% decrease) = 85 × (1 − 40/100) = 85 × 0.60 = 51

\n

⇒ Price after 3rd month (60% decrease) = 51 × (1 − 60/100) = 51 × 0.40 = 20.4

\n

⇒ Price after 4th month (45% increase) = 20.4 × (1 + 45/100) = 20.4 × 1.45 = 29.58

\n

⇒ Total decrease in price = Original Price − Final Price

\n

⇒ Total decrease = 100 − 29.58 = 70.42

\n

⇒ Percentage decrease = (70.42 ÷ 100) × 100 = 70.42%

\n

∴ The correct answer is Decreases by 70.42%.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Successive Percentage Change

\n

If a value changes successively by a% and b%, the net percentage change can be calculated using the formula: Net Change = a + b + (a × b) ÷ 100. For a decrease, always use a negative sign.

\n

Multiplying Factor Method

\n

Instead of computing the percentage and subtracting, directly multiply the base by (1 ± R/100). For example, a 15% decrease uses a factor of 0.85, and a 45% increase uses 1.45. This method is exceptionally fast for successive changes.

\n

Ratio Method for Successive Changes

\n

Convert percentages to fractions. A 40% decrease (−2/5) leaves 3/5 of the value. The overall change ratio is simply the product of all individual fractional multipliers.

दिलेले आहे:

\n\n

मूळ किंमत = ₹100 (सरलीकरणासाठी गृहीत धरा)

\n\n

वापरलेले सूत्र:

\n\n

क्रमवार घट: नवीन किंमत = जुनी किंमत × (1 - शेकडा घट)

\n\n

क्रमवार वाढ: नवीन किंमत = जुनी किंमत × (1 + शेकडा वाढ)

\n\n

शेकडा बदल = [(अंतिम किंमत - मूळ किंमत)/मूळ किंमत] \\( \\times 100 \\)

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

1ल्या महिन्यानंतर = 100 × 0.85 = ₹85

\n\n

2ऱ्या महिन्यानंतर = 85 × 0.60 = ₹51

\n\n

3ऱ्या महिन्यानंतर = 51 × 0.40 = ₹20.4

\n\n

4थ्या महिन्यानंतर = 20.4 × 1.45 = ₹29.58

\n\n

शेकडा बदल = ((29.58 − 100) ÷ 100)  x  100 = −70.42%

\n\n

∴ निव्वळ बदल = मूळ किंमतीच्या तुलनेत 70.42% घट

ଦିଆଯାଇଛି:

\n\n

ମୂଳ ମୂଲ୍ୟ = ₹100 (ସରଳତା ପାଇଁ ଅନୁମାନ କରାଯାଇଛି)

\n\n

\n\n

କ୍ରମାଗତ ହ୍ରାସ: ନୂତନ ମୂଲ୍ୟ = ପୁରୁଣା ମୂଲ୍ୟ x (1 - % ହ୍ରାସ)

\n\n

କ୍ରମାଗତ ବୃଦ୍ଧି: ନୂତନ ମୂଲ୍ୟ = ପୁରୁଣା ମୂଲ୍ୟ x (1 + % ବୃଦ୍ଧି)

\n\n

% ପରିବର୍ତ୍ତନ = \\(\\frac{\\text{ଅନ୍ତିମ ମୂଲ୍ୟ} - \\text{ମୂଳ ମୂଲ୍ୟ}}{\\text{ମୂଳ ମୂଲ୍ୟ}} \\times 100 \\)

\n\n

ଗଣନା:

\n\n

ପ୍ରଥମ ମାସ ପରେ = 100 x 0.85 = ₹85

\n\n

ଦ୍ୱିତୀୟ ମାସ ପରେ = 85 x 0.60 = ₹51

\n\n

ତୃତୀୟ ମାସ ପରେ = 51 x 0.40 = ₹20.4

\n\n

ଚତୁର୍ଥ ମାସ ପରେ = 20.4 x 1.45 = ₹29.58

\n\n

% ପରିବର୍ତ୍ତନ = ((29.58 − 100) ÷ 100) x 100 = −70.42%

\n\n

∴ ନେଟ୍ ପରିବର୍ତ୍ତନ = ମୂଳ ମୂଲ୍ୟ ତୁଳନାରେ 70.42% ହ୍ରାସ

\n", "as": "", "ks": "", "kok": "", "mni": "", "ne": "", "pa": "

ਦਿੱਤਾ ਗਿਆ ਹੈ:

\n\n

ਮੂਲ ਕੀਮਤ = ₹100 (ਸਰਲਤਾ ਲਈ ਮੰਨਿਆ ਗਿਆ)

\n\n

ਵਰਤਿਆ ਗਿਆ ਸੂਤਰ:

\n\n

ਲਗਾਤਾਰ ਕਮੀ: ਨਵੀਂ ਕੀਮਤ = ਪੁਰਾਣੀ ਕੀਮਤ x (1 - % ਕਮੀ)

\n\n

ਲਗਾਤਾਰ ਵਾਧਾ: ਨਵੀਂ ਕੀਮਤ = ਪੁਰਾਣੀ ਕੀਮਤ x (1 + % ਵਾਧਾ)

\n\n

% ਪਰਿਵਰਤਨ = \\(\\frac{\\text{ਅੰਤਿਮ ਕੀਮਤ} - \\text{ਮੂਲ ਕੀਮਤ}}{\\text{ਮੂਲ ਕੀਮਤ}} \\times 100 \\)

\n\n

ਗਣਨਾ:

\n\n

ਪਹਿਲੇ ਮਹੀਨੇ ਤੋਂ ਬਾਅਦ = 100 x 0.85 = ₹85

\n\n

ਦੂਜੇ ਮਹੀਨੇ ਤੋਂ ਬਾਅਦ = 85 x 0.60 = ₹51

\n\n

ਤੀਜੇ ਮਹੀਨੇ ਤੋਂ ਬਾਅਦ = 51 x 0.40 = ₹20.4

\n\n

ਚੌਥੇ ਮਹੀਨੇ ਤੋਂ ਬਾਅਦ = 20.4 x 1.45 = ₹29.58

\n\n

% ਤਬਦੀਲੀ = ((29.58 − 100) ÷ 100) x 100 = −70.42%

\n\n

∴ ਕੁੱਲ ਪਰਿਵਰਤਨ = ਅਸਲ ਕੀਮਤ ਦੇ ਮੁਕਾਬਲੇ 70.42% ਕਮੀ

Shortcut Trick\n

SP for 100 pears = CP of 100 pears + Profit per 100 pears

\n

SP for 100 pears = 1230 + (980 ÷ 4) = 1230 + 245 = ₹1475

\n

SP for 1 pear = 1475 ÷ 100 = ₹14.75

\n

SP for 1 dozen (12 pears) = 14.75 × 12 = ₹177

\n

∴ The correct answer is ₹177.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n\n

Given:

\n

Total pears bought = 400

\n

Cost Price (CP) of 100 pears = ₹1230

\n

Total profit on 400 pears = ₹980

\n

1 Dozen = 12 units

\n\n

Formula Used:

\n

Total Selling Price (SP) = Total CP + Total Profit

\n

{"en": "\n

Shortcut Trick\n

A ratio acts as a fraction where the first term is the numerator and the second is the denominator.

\n

If Numerator > Denominator, the ratio's value is greater than 1.

\n

If Numerator < Denominator, the ratio's value is less than 1.

\n

In the given options, only 19 : 13 has a numerator greater than its denominator (19 > 13).

\n

Therefore, 19 : 13 is strictly greater than 1, instantly making it the highest among all proper fraction options.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 19 : 13.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Four ratios: 15 : 19, 19 : 13, 12 : 19, and 21 : 26

\n

Concept Used:

\n

Convert each ratio to its decimal equivalent to compare their exact scale values.

\n\n\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ 15 : 19 = 15 ÷ 19 ≈ 0.789

\n

⇒ 19 : 13 = 19 ÷ 13 ≈ 1.461

\n

⇒ 12 : 19 = 12 ÷ 19 ≈ 0.631

\n

⇒ 21 : 26 = 21 ÷ 26 ≈ 0.807

\n

⇒ Comparing the decimal values: 1.461 > 0.807 > 0.789 > 0.631

\n

⇒ The highest decimal value correctly corresponds to the ratio 19 : 13.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 19 : 13.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Proper vs Improper Fractions

\n

A proper fraction has a numerator smaller than its denominator, ensuring its overall value is strictly less than 1. An improper fraction features a larger numerator, making its overall value directly greater than 1.

\n\n

Cross-Multiplication Method

\n

To effectively compare two fractions a/b and c/d without calculating decimals, cross-multiply. If (a × d) > (b × c), then it is proven that a/b > c/d.

\n\n

Compound Ratio

\n

The compound ratio of two individual ratios a : b and c : d is mathematically defined as ac : bd. It basically represents the direct multiplication of the two given fractions.

"end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "1", "solution_html": {"en": "\n

Shortcut Trick\n

For 2 years, Principal = (SI ÷ 2)2 ÷ (CI − SI)

\n

Given SI = ₹44 and CI = ₹45

\n

Principal = (44 ÷ 2)2 ÷ (45 − 44)

\n

Principal = 222 ÷ 1 = 484

\n

∴ The correct answer is ₹484.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Simple interest (SI) for 2 years = ₹44

\n

Compound interest (CI) for 2 years = ₹45

\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ Simple interest for 1 year = 44 ÷ 2 = ₹22

\n

⇒ Compound interest for 1st year = ₹22

\n

⇒ Compound interest for 2nd year = Total CI − CI for 1st year

\n

⇒ CI for 2nd year = 45 − 22 = ₹23

\n

⇒ Difference in 2nd year interest = 23 − 22 = ₹1

\n

⇒ This ₹1 is the interest earned on 1st year's interest (₹22) at rate R.

\n

⇒ Rate (R) = (1 × 100) ÷ 22 = (50 ÷ 11)%

\n

⇒ We know, SI = (P × R × T) ÷ 100

\n

⇒ 22 = (P × (50 ÷ 11) × 1) ÷ 100

\n

⇒ P = (22 × 100 × 11) ÷ 50

\n

⇒ P = 22 × 2 × 11 = 484

\n

∴ The correct answer is ₹484.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Difference between CI and SI for 2 years:

\n

D = P(R ÷ 100)2, where P is the principal and R is the rate of interest.

\n

Difference between CI and SI for 3 years:

\n

D = P(R ÷ 100)2(3 + R ÷ 100).

\n

SI vs CI:

\n

Simple interest is calculated only on the principal amount, whereas compound interest is calculated on the principal and also on the accumulated interest of previous periods.

ਵਰਤਿਆ ਗਿਆ ਸੂਤਰ:

\n\n

ਸਧਾਰਨ ਵਿਆਜ (SI) = (ਮੂਲ x ਦਰ x ਸਮਾਂ) / 100

\n\n

ਮਿਸ਼ਰਿਤ ਵਿਆਜ (CI) = ਮੂਲ x [(1 + ਦਰ / 100)ਸਮਾਂ − 1]

\n\n

ਗਣਨਾ:

\n\n

ਮੰਨ ਲਓ ਮੂਲ = ₹P ਅਤੇ ਦਰ = R%।

\n\n

SI ਫਾਰਮੂਲੇ ਤੋਂ: SI = (P x R x 2) / 100

\n\n

⇒ ₹44 = (P x R x 2) / 100

\n\n

⇒ P x R = ₹2200

\n\n

CI ਸੂਤਰ ਤੋਂ: CI = P x [(1 + R / 100)2 − 1]

\n\n

⇒ ₹45 = P x [(1 + R / 100)2 − 1]

\n\n

P x R = ₹2200 ਨੂੰ CI ਸਮੀਕਰਨ ਵਿੱਚ ਸ਼ਾਮਲ ਕਰੋ:

\n\n

₹45 = P x [(1 + R / 100)2 − 1]

\n\n

⇒ ₹45 = P x [(1 + (2200 / P x 100))2 − 1]

\n\n

P ਲਈ ਹੱਲ ਕਰਨ ਤੋਂ ਬਾਅਦ:

\n\n

ਮੂਲ (P) = ₹484

\n\n

ਮੂਲ ₹484 ਹੈ।

Shortcut Trick\n

The smallest number divisible by the given numbers is their LCM.

\n

Notice that 48 is already a multiple of 12 and 24, so we can ignore 12 and 24.

\n

We just need to find the LCM of 48 and 58.

\n

Both 48 and 58 are divisible by 2: 48 = 24 × 2 and 58 = 29 × 2.

\n

LCM(48, 58) = 24 × 29 × 2 = 48 × 29 = 1392.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 1392.

\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

The numbers are 24, 58, 48, and 12.

\n

Concept Used:

\n

The smallest natural number exactly divisible by a given set of numbers is the Least Common Multiple (LCM) of those numbers.

\n

Calculations:

\n

Find the prime factorization of each number:

\n

⇒ 12 = 22 × 3

\n

⇒ 24 = 23 × 3

\n

⇒ 48 = 24 × 3

\n

⇒ 58 = 21 × 29

\n

To find the LCM, take the highest power of each prime factor present:

\n

⇒ LCM = 24 × 31 × 291

\n

⇒ LCM = 16 × 3 × 29

\n

⇒ LCM = 48 × 29

\n

⇒ LCM = 1392

\n

∴ The correct answer is 1392.

\n

Additional Information

\n

Least Common Multiple (LCM)

\n

The LCM of two or more numbers is the smallest possible number that is a multiple of each of the given numbers.

\n

Highest Common Factor (HCF)

\n

The HCF of two or more numbers is the greatest number that divides each of them exactly without leaving any remainder.

\n

LCM and HCF Relationship

\n

For any two specific numbers a and b, the product of their LCM and HCF is equal to the product of the numbers themselves: LCM(a, b) × HCF(a, b) = a × b.

\n

Co-prime Numbers

\n

Two numbers are said to be co-prime if their HCF is 1. The LCM of any two co-prime numbers is simply their product.

Shortcut Trick\n

When amounts are equal at CI, ratio of principal shares is P1/P2 = (1 + R/100)t2 − t1.

\n

Difference in time for Rishi and Shan = 18 − 17 = 1 year.

\n

Rishi's share ÷ Shan's share = (100 + 8) ÷ 100 = 108/100 = 27/25.

{"prompt": "2", "value": "875"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "775"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "687"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "1", "solution_html": {"en": "\n

Shortcut Trick\n

When amounts are equal at CI, ratio of principal shares is P1/P2 = (1 + R/100)t2 − t1.

\n

Difference in time for Rishi and Shan = 18 − 17 = 1 year.

\n

Rishi's share ÷ Shan's share = (100 + 8) ÷ 100 = 108/100 = 27/25.

\n

Total parts = 27 + 25 = 52 parts ≡ ₹1612 ⇒ 1 part = ₹31.

\n

Rishi's share = 27 × 31 = ₹837.

\n

∴ The correct answer is ₹837.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Total sum Pravin has = ₹1612

\n

Rate of interest (R) = 8% compounded annually

\n

Time for Rishi (t1) = 17 years

\n

Time for Shan (t2) = 18 years

\n\n

Formula Used:

\n

A = P(1 + R/100)n

\n\n\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ Let the amount given to Rishi be x and to Shan be y.

\n

⇒ x + y = 1612

\n

Since the amounts received by both are equal:

\n

⇒ Amount of Rishi = Amount of Shan

\n

⇒ x × (1 + 8/100)17 = y × (1 + 8/100)18

\n

⇒ x / y = (1 + 8/100)18 − 17

\n

⇒ x / y = (1 + 8/100)1

\n

⇒ x / y = 108 / 100

\n

⇒ x / y = 27 / 25

\n

⇒ Total ratio parts = 27 + 25 = 52 parts

\n

⇒ 52 parts = 1612

\n

⇒ 1 part = 1612 ÷ 52 = 31

\n

⇒ Amount given to Rishi (x) = 27 parts

\n

⇒ 27 × 31 = 837

\n

∴ The correct answer is ₹837.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Compound Interest Formula

\n

The amount after n years at compound interest is given by A = P(1 + R/100)n, where P is the principal and R is the rate.

\n

Equal Amounts Distribution

\n

If a total sum is split into P1 and P2 such that their amounts are equal after t1 and t2 years, then the ratio of their investments is P1 / P2 = (1 + R/100)t2 − t1.

\n

Difference Between CI and SI

\n

For the first year, CI and SI are exactly the same. For 2 years, the difference between CI and SI on a certain sum is given by D = P(R/100)2.

\n67:T14fc,\n&l", "hn": "\n

दिया गया है:

\n\n

प्रवीण के पास ₹1612 हैं।

\n\n

ऋषि का निवेश 17 वर्षों के लिए वार्षिक चक्रवृद्धि ब्याज अर्जित करता है।

\n\n

शान का निवेश 18 वर्षों के लिए वार्षिक चक्रवृद्धि ब्याज अर्जित करता है।

\n\n

ब्याज की दर 8% प्रति वर्ष है।

\n\n

ऋषि और शान दोनों को अपनी-अपनी अवधि के बाद समान राशि प्राप्त होती है।

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

चक्रवृद्धि ब्याज सूत्र: भविष्य का मूल्य (FV) = मूलधन (P) × (1 + दर/100)समय

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

मान लीजिए कि ऋषि को दी गई राशि ₹x है।

\n\n

तब, शान को दी गई राशि ₹(1612 - x) होगी।

\n\n

17 वर्षों के बाद ऋषि के निवेश का भविष्य मूल्य = 18 वर्षों के बाद शान के निवेश का भविष्य मूल्य।

\n\n

⇒ x × (1 + 8/100)17 = (1612 - x) × (1 + 8/100)18

\n\n

⇒ x × 1.0817 = (1612 - x) × 1.0818

\n\n

⇒ x × 1.0817 = 1612 × 1.0818 - x × 1.0818

\n\n

⇒ x × (1.0817 + 1.0818) = 1612 × 1.0818

\n\n

⇒ x × 1.0817 × (1 + 1.08) = 1612 × 1.0818

\n\n

⇒ x × 1.0817 × 2.08 = 1612 × 1.0818

\n\n

⇒ x = (1612 × 1.0818) / (1.0817 × 2.08)

\n\n

⇒ x = (1612 × 1.08) / 2.08

\n\n

⇒ x = 1739.296 / 2.08

\n\n

⇒ x ≈ 837

\n\n

प्रवीण ने ऋषि को ₹837 दिए थे।

ఇచ్చినది:

\n\n

ప్రవీణ్ దగ్గర ₹1612 ఉన్నాయి.

\n\n

प्रवीणजवळ ₹1612 आहेत.

\n\n

ऋषीची गुंतवणूक 17 वर्षांसाठी वार्षिक चक्रवाढ व्याजाने वाढते.

\n\n

शॉनची गुंतवणूक 18 वर्षांसाठी वार्षिक चक्रवाढ व्याजाने वाढते.

\n\n

व्याज दर वार्षिक 8% आहे.

\n\n

ऋषी आणि शॉन दोघांनाही त्यांच्या संबंधित कालावधीनंतर समान राशी मिळते.

\n\n

वापरलेले सूत्र:

\n\n

चक्रवाढ व्याजाचे सूत्र: भविष्यकालीन मूल्य (FV) = मुद्दल (P) × (1 + दर/100)मुदत

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

समजा, ऋषीला दिलेली रक्कम ₹x आहे.

\n\n

तर शॉनला दिलेली रक्कम ₹(1612 - x) असेल.

\n\n

17 वर्षांनंतर ऋषीच्या गुंतवणुकीचे भविष्यकालीन मूल्य = 18 वर्षांनंतर शॉनच्या गुंतवणुकीचे भविष्यकालीन मूल्य.

\n\n

⇒ x × (1 + 8/100)17 = (1612 - x) × (1 + 8/100)18

\n\n

⇒ x × 1.0817 = (1612 - x) × 1.0818

\n\n

⇒ x × 1.0817 = 1612 × 1.0818 - x × 1.0818

\n\n

⇒ x × (1.0817 + 1.0818) = 1612 × 1.0818

\n\n

⇒ x × 1.0817 × (1 + 1.08) = 1612 × 1.0818

\n\n

⇒ x × 1.0817 × 2.08 = 1612 × 1.0818

\n\n

⇒ x = (1612 × 1.0818) / (1.0817 × 2.08)

\n\n

⇒ x = (1612 × 1.08) / 2.08

\n\n

⇒ x = 1739.296 / 2.08

\n\n

⇒ x ≈ 837

\n\n

கூட்டு வட்டி சூத்திரம்: எதிர்கால மதிப்பு (FV) = அசல் (P) x (1 + விகிதம்/100)காலம்

\n\n

கணக்கீடு:

\n\n

ரிஷிக்கு கொடுக்கப்பட்ட தொகை ₹x ஆக இருக்கட்டும்.

\n\n

அப்படியானால் ஷானுக்கு கொடுக்கப்பட்ட தொகை ₹(1612 - x) ஆக இருக்கும்.

\n\n

17 ஆண்டுகளுக்குப் பிறகு ரிஷியின் முதலீட்டின் எதிர்கால மதிப்பு = 18 ஆண்டுகளுக்குப் பிறகு ஷானின் முதலீட்டின் எதிர்கால மதிப்பு.

\n\n

⇒ x x (1 + 8/100)17 = (1612 - x) x (1 + 8/100)18

\n\n

⇒ x x 1.0817 = (1612 - x) x 1.0818

\n\n

⇒ x x 1.0817 = 1612 x 1.0818 - x x 1.0818

\n\n

⇒ x x (1.0817 + 1.0818) = 1612 x 1.0818

\n\n

⇒ x x 1.0817 x (1 + 1.08) = 1612 x 1.0818

\n\n

⇒ x x 1.0817 x 2.08 = 1612 x 1.0818

\n\n

⇒ x = (1612 x 1.0818) / (1.0817 x 2.08)

\n\n

⇒ x = (1612 x 1.08) / 2.08

\n\n

⇒ x = 1739.296 / 2.08

\n\n

⇒ x ≈ 837

Shortcut Trick\n

Average speed is the ratio of total distance travelled to total time taken.

\n

Total Distance = 150 + 250 = 400 km

\n

Total Time = 4 + 4 = 8 hours

\n

Average Speed = 400 ÷ 8 = 50 km/hr

\n

∴ The correct answer is 50 km/hr.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Distance 1 (D1) = 150 km

\n

Time 1 (T1) = 4 hours

\n

Distance 2 (D2) = 250 km

\n

Time 2 (T2) = 4 hours

\n\n

Formula Used:

\n

Average Speed = Total Distance ÷ Total Time

\n\n\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ Total Distance = D1 + D2 = 150 + 250 = 400 km

\n

⇒ Total Time = T1 + T2 = 4 + 4 = 8 hours

\n

⇒ Average Speed = 400 ÷ 8

\n

⇒ Average Speed = 50 km/hr

\n

∴ The correct answer is 50 km/hr.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Average Speed (Equal Distances)

\n

When a person travels two equal distances at speeds x and y, Average Speed = (2xy) ÷ (x + y).

\n\n

Average Speed (Equal Time)

\n

When a person travels for two equal time periods at speeds x and y, Average Speed = (x + y) ÷ 2.

\n\n

Speed Unit Conversion

\n

To convert km/hr to m/s, multiply by 5 ÷ 18. To convert m/s to km/hr, multiply by 18 ÷ 5.

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

మొదటి భాగం దూరం = 150 కి.మీ.

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

ఉపయోగించిన సూత్రం:

\n\n

సగటు వేగం = మొత్తం దూరం / మొత్తం సమయం

\n\n

గణన:

\n\n

మొత్తం దూరం = 150 + 250 = 400 కి.మీ.

\n\n

మొత్తం సమయం = 4 + 4 = 8 గంటలు

\n\n

సగటు వేగం = మొత్తం దూరం / మొత్తం సమయం

\n\n

⇒ సగటు వేగం = 400 / 8

\n\n

⇒ సగటు వేగం = 50 కి.మీ./గం

\n\n

సంజయ్ సగటు వేగం 50 కి.మీ./గం.

\n\n

दुसऱ्या भागासाठी लागणारा वेळ = 4 तास

\n\n

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

\n\n

एकूण वेळ = 4 + 4 = 8 तास

\n\n

\n\n

கொடுக்கப்பட்டவை:

\n\n

முதல் பகுதி தூரம் = 150 கி.மீ

\n\n

முதல் பகுதிக்கு எடுத்துக்கொண்ட நேரம் = 4 மணிநேரம்

\n\n

இரண்டாம் பகுதி தூரம் = 250 கி.மீ

\n\n

இரண்டாம் பகுதிக்கு எடுத்துக்கொண்ட நேரம் = 4 மணிநேரம்

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

ପ୍ରଥମ ଭାଗ ପାଇଁ ନିଆଯାଇଥିବା ସମୟ = 4 ଘଣ୍ଟା

\n\n

ଦ୍ୱିତୀୟ ଭାଗର ଦୂରତା = 250 କିମି

\n\n

ଦ୍ୱିତୀୟ ଭାଗ ପାଇଁ ନିଆଯାଇଥିବା ସମୟ = 4 ଘଣ୍ଟା

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

ਦਿੱਤਾ ਗਿਆ:

\n\n

ਪਹਿਲੇ ਭਾਗ ਦੀ ਦੂਰੀ = 150 ਕਿਲੋਮੀਟਰ

\n\n

ਪਹਿਲੇ ਭਾਗ ਲਈ ਲੱਗਿਆ ਸਮਾਂ = 4 ਘੰਟੇ

\n\n

ਦੂਜੇ ਭਾਗ ਦੀ ਦੂਰੀ = 250 ਕਿਲੋਮੀਟਰ

\n\n

ਦੂਜੇ ਭਾਗ ਲਈ ਲੱਗਿਆ ਸਮਾਂ = 4 ਘੰਟੇ

\n\n

ਵਰਤਿਆ ਗਿਆ :

\n\n

ਔਸਤ ਗਤੀ = ਕੁੱਲ ਤੈਅ ਕੀਤੀ ਦੂਰੀ / ਕੁੱਲ ਲੱਗਿਆ ਸਮਾਂ

\n\n

ਗਣਨਾ:

\n\n

ਕੁੱਲ ਦੂਰੀ = 150 + 250 = 400 ਕਿਲੋਮੀਟਰ

\n\n

ਕੁੱਲ ਸਮਾਂ = 4 + 4 = 8 ਘੰਟੇ

\n\n

ਔਸਤ ਗਤੀ = ਕੁੱਲ ਦੂਰੀ / ਕੁੱਲ ਸਮਾਂ

\n\n

⇒ ਔਸਤ ਗਤੀ = 400 / 8

\n\n

⇒ ਔਸਤ ਗਤੀ = 50 ਕਿਲੋਮੀਟਰ/ਘੰਟਾ

\n\n

ਸੰਜੇ ਦੀ ਔਸਤ ਗਤੀ 50 ਕਿਲੋਮੀਟਰ/ਘੰਟਾ ਹੈ।
